def proj_grad_step(h, beta, max_iters, t0, deadline):
   f = smoothobjective(h, beta)
   best_h, best_f = h, f
   best_c_raw = max(correlate)
   for it in range(max_iters):
       if time: break
       q = correlate(h,1-h); p=softmax(beta*(q-maxq)); g=grad_from_weights(p,h,n)
       # if grad small break
       # line search: try step t starting maybe last_t
       accepted=False
       for ls in range(30):
           ht = project(h - t*g, T)
           ft, qt = ...; if ft < f - 1e-13 or it early? 
               h=ht; f=ft; best update; accepted=True; break
           t*=0.5
       if not accepted: break
```

def smooth_value(x, n, alpha):
   q = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x)
   mx = q.max()
   u = alpha*(q-mx)
   # if alpha=0? use max
   s = np.exp(u).sum()
   if s==0... 
   return mx + np.log(s)/alpha
```
Grad:
```
def smooth_grad(x, n, alpha):
   q=...
   mx=q.max()
   u=alpha*(q-mx)
   p=np.exp(u); p/=p.sum()
   ones=np.ones(n)
   A=np.convolve(p, ones, 'full')[N:N+n] # p indexed offset? p length 2n-1 corresponds s=N to -N. convolve formula requires p order? Let's verify slicing.
   D1=np.convolve(p, x, 'full')[N:N+n]
   D2=np.convolve(p[::-1], x, 'full')[N:N+n]
   return A - D1 - D2
```
Need ensure p order corresponds s from -(n-1) to +(n-1) or reversed? correlate output index m=0 corresponds shift -(n-1) (most negative). Let's test with n small to confirm sign. We need q array from correlate. q[0] corresponds s=-N: ∑ x_i (1-x_{i+N})? Let's check NumPy correlate convention: `correlate(a, v)` computes `sum a[n+k] * conj(v[n])`? Let's recall from docs: For method='full', returns `c_k = sum_n a_{n+k} v_n^*` maybe with c from -(M-1) to N-1? Actually numpy.polynomial? Let's test mentally with arrays [a,b], [g0,g1]. Full correlation of a with v: v_corr? In FFT, correlate is convolution of a with reversed v. np.correlate(a, v, 'full') = np.convolve(a, v[::-1], 'full'). For a=[a,b], v=[g0,g1], reversed [g1,g0]. convolve [a,b] with [g1,g0] full length3: index0: a*g1; index1: a*g0 + b*g1; index2: b*g0. Thus q[0]=a*g1 = h0(1-h1); q[1]=h0*h0? Actually a*g0 = h0(1-h0), b*g1=h1(1-h2? for n=2 g1=1-b) => h0(1-h0)+h1(1-h1); q[2]=b*g0=h1(1-h0). Wait q[1] diagonal both, q[0] cross h0*(1-h1), q[2] h1*(1-h0). Now derivative formula q_s(s negative) earlier: For s=-1, q=∑ h_i(1-h_{i+1}). Derivative at h_j: positive if j as h_i (1-h_{j+1}) valid j+1<n; negative if j as h_{i+1}: -h_{j-1} valid j-1≥0. Formula positive (1-h_{j-s}) with s=-1 -> 1-h_{j+1}; negative -h_{j+s}= -h_{j-1}. Good. So D1 = ∑ p_s h[j-s]? For s=-1: h[j+1], which is the negative term? Wait D1 formula should be h[j-s] for positive term? Positive term uses h_{j-s}: s=-1 -> h_{j+1}, but derivative positive term is 1 - h_{j+1}. So A - D1 contributes -(h[j+1]), good. D2 = ∑ p_s h[j+s]: s=-1 -> h[j-1], negative term -h[j-1]. So grad = A - D1 - D2. Good.

But A = ∑ p_s 1_{0≤j-s<n}. For s=-1: j+1<n -> j<n-1. Convolution p*ones: A[j]=∑ p_s ones[j-s] (ones index j-s). For s=-1: ones[j+1] valid if j+1<n. Good. D1=conv p*x: ∑ p_s x[j-s], s=-1 -> x[j+1]. D2=conv p_rev*x: p_rev index t with p_{-t}; D2[j]=∑ p_rev[t] x[j-t]=∑ p_{-t} x[j-t]. Let s=-t => ∑ p_s x[j+s]. Good. Perfect.

L-BFGS-B penalty:
```
def penalized_fun(x, n, alpha, nu):
    return smooth_value(x,n,alpha) + 0.5*nu*(x.sum()-n/2)^2
def penalized_jac(x, n, alpha, nu):
    g = smooth_grad(x,n,alpha)
    dev = x.sum()-n/2
    if nu !=0: g = g + nu*dev*np.ones(n)
    return g
```

```
def active_optimize(h, deadline, max_rounds=...):
  best=h; raw=...
  for round in range(max_rounds):
     q=correlate(best,1-best); mx=q.max()
     tol = max(1e-6, 0.1? raw spread?)
     p = (q >= mx - tol).astype(float); p/=sum
     # maybe use smoothing p = softmax(beta*(mx - q))? 
     res = minimize(fixed_weight_objective, best, jac, bounds, args=(n,p,nu), maxiter=50)
     h2=project(res.x)
     raw2=max(correlate(h2,1-h2))
     if raw2 < raw - eps: best=h2; raw=raw2; good
     else: if this round no improve, decrease tol? or break
```
Since fixed p, objective gradient uses p directly; no alpha. This may find descent for current active set. But if p includes too many lags, G_p may increase non-active lags. Let's design `project_box_sum` using bisection. Need handle T=n/2.
```
def project_box_sum(x, n):
    T = 0.5*n
    # Initial x may contain NaN? no
    lo = -np.max(x) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - np.min(x) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid=(lo+hi)/2
        s=np.clip(x+mid,0,1).sum()
        if s < T: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    y=np.clip(x+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust tiny residual
    diff=T-y.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # distribute to under/over with small tol
       if diff>0:
          room=1-y; idx=np.where(room>1e-12)[0]; if len: y[idx]+=diff/len(idx)
       else: idx=np.where(y>1e-12)[0]; ...
    return y
```

Let's design `lbfgs_smooth(h0, n, alphas, nus, maxiter, deadline)`:
```
best_h, best_qraw = h0, max(compute_q)
bounds = [(0,1)]*n
for alpha in alphas:
  if time>deadline: break
  for nu in nus:
    if time>deadline-? break
    try:
       res = minimize(pen_fun, best_h, jac=pen_jac, args=(n,alpha,nu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                      options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-18, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxls':20, 'maxcor':10})
       h2 = project_box_sum(res.x if success else best_h, T)
       qraw = max(q)
       if qraw < best_qraw - 1e-8:
           best_h, best_qraw = h2, qraw
    except Exception: pass
```

Pseudo for CD:
```
def exact_cd(h, n, deadline, max_passes, rng, min_improve=1e-12):
    h=h.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h); best=max(c)
    for pass:
       improved=False
       order random or sequential
       for p in order:
          old=h[p]
          if old <=1e-10 or old>=1-1e-10: continue? Maybe boundaries can move.
          new=solve_1d(c,h,p,n)
          if abs(new-old)>1e-10:
             update_corr(c,h,p,old,new,n); h[p]=new; improved=True
       if not improved: break
       # after pass: project to sum? project_box_sum fast? But c inconsistent. If sum drift > 1e-7, recompute c.
       if abs(h.sum()-n/2)>1e-8:
          h=project_box_sum(h,n)
          c=np.correlate(h,1-h)
       # compute best maybe every pass
       b=max(c)
       if b<best: best=b
    return h, best*dx
```

```

Feasibility(L):
```
if L < unaff + tol: return False
# affected ms array length K up to 2n-1
# non-diag c_base, a. Need find x interval from linear constraints and [0,1].
xl=0,xh=1
if any a==0 and c_base > L: infeasible
if any a<0:
    vals = (L - c_base)/a + old  # since a neg, lower bound
    xl = max(xl, vals.max())
if any a>0:
    vals = (L - c_base)/a + old  # upper bound
    xh = min(xh, vals.min())
# clamp xl,xh to [0,1]. If xl > xh + tol infeasible.
# diagonal: if exists, U = L - c_diag + old*(1-old)
# We need interval [xl,xh] subset where x(1-x) ≤ U.
# Equivalent maxparab(xl,xh) ≤ U.
```

Coordinate update function:
```
def coord_new(c,h,p,n):
   N=n-1
   lo = min(p, N-p)
   hi = max(N+p, 2*N-p)
   ms = np.arange(lo,hi+1)
   d = N - ms
   a = np.zeros(len(ms))
   diag_pos = np.where(ms==N)[0]
   # first factor: p+d valid
   fp = p + d
   vf = (fp>=0)&(fp<n)
   a[vf] += (1.0 - h[fp[vf]])
   # second: p-d valid
   sp = p - d
   vs = (sp>=0)&(sp<n)
   a[vs] -= h[sp[vs]]
   cbase = c[ms].copy()
   # set diag a=0
   if diag_pos: a[diag_pos]=0; c_diag = c[ms[N-N?]]
   # masks nonzero a
   pos = (a > 1e-12) & (np.arange len not diag?) but diag a=0
   neg = (a < -1e-12)
   zero = (a <=1e-12)&(a>=-1e-12)
   unaff = max(np.max(c[:lo]) if lo>0 else -inf, np.max(c[hi+1:]) if hi+1<len else -inf)
   low = max(0.0, unaff)
   high = float(np.max(c))
   # binary search
   for _ in range(50):
      L=0.5*(low+high)
      if feasible(L): high=L else low=L
   L=high
   # compute interval and choose x
```
Feasibility can be a nested function? Rule no closures/lambdas. Need top-level helper with arguments. To avoid closure, implement feasible logic inside `solve_coord` directly and define a nested? Closures prohibited. We can define top-level `coord_feasible(L, lo, hi, c, a, cbase, old, unaff, diag_idx, c_diag, has_diag)` but passing many arrays each iteration slow. Could write top-level `coord_search` that loops. We can define helper functions without closures, but nested local not closure? The rules: "Make all helper functions top level, no closures or lambdas". We can define all top-level. Within solve, to avoid creating function, just inline binary feasibility in a loop using vectorized conditions.

Inlining:
```
low = max(0.0, unaff if unaff>0 else 0.0)
high = cur_max
for _ in range(50):
    L = 0.5*(low+high)

   N=n-1; lo=min(p,N-p); hi=max(N+p,2*N-p); ms=np.arange(lo,hi+1); d=N-ms

CD loop with chunks. We need `coords` precomputed. Function:
```
def exact_cd(h,n,deadline,coords,passes,chunk,rng):
  h=h.copy(); c=compute_q(h)
  best_raw = float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy()
  target = n/2.0
  order = list(range(n))
  for it in range(passes):
     if time.time()>deadline: break
     if rng is not None and it%2==1: rng.shuffle(order)
     # iterate in chunks
     for start in range(0,n,chunk):
        for p in order[start:start+chunk]:
           old=h[p]
           if old <= 1e-12 or old >= 1-1e-12? Could still move out from boundary; but if at 0, new maybe >0. Don't skip? If old=0, diagonal quadratic, can move. But coordinate solve handles. However if old at bound, x may remain. Keep.
           new, ms, a, diag = solve_coord(c,h,p,coords)
           if abs(new-old)>1e-12:
               apply_update(c,h,p,old,new,ms,a,diag)
               h[p]=new
        # after chunk, project sum, recompute c if changed
        if abs(h.sum()-target) > 1e-8:
            h = project_box_sum(h, target)
            c = compute_q(h)
        else:
            # c may still be accurate? coordinate updates summed and h sum slightly; c updated for each coordinate. okay.
            pass
     cur_raw = float(c.max())
     if cur_raw < best_raw:
        best_raw = cur_raw; best_h=h.copy()
     # Optional break if no significant improvement? track
  return best_h, best_raw
```

Smooth objective:
```
def smoothfun(x,n,alpha,nu):
   q=np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full')
   mx=q.max()
   u=alpha*(q-mx)
   # if alpha infinite? use max? If alpha very large, u underflow to -inf for many; sum ~1; log ~0. okay.
   s=np.exp(u).sum()
   if s <=0: return mx + 0.5*nu*(x.sum()-n/2)**2
   return mx + math.log(s)/alpha + 0.5*nu*(x.sum()-n/2)**2
```
Need import math.
Gradient:
```
def smoothgrad(x,n,alpha,nu):
   q=...
   mx=q.max(); u=alpha*(q-mx); p=np.exp(u); s=p.sum()
   if s==0: p = np.zeros; p[argmax]=1
   else p/=s
   N=n-1
   ones=np.ones(n)
   A=np.convolve(p, ones, 'full')[N:N+n]
   D1=np.convolve(p, x, 'full')[N:N+n]
   D2=np.convolve(p[::-1], x, 'full')[N:N+n]
   g=A-D1-D2
   if nu:
      g += nu*(x.sum()-n/2)*np.ones(n)
   return g
```

    dev=x.sum()-0.5*n
    return val + 0.5*nu*dev*dev

def jac_pen(x,n,alpha,nu):
    g=smoothgrad(x,n,alpha,0.0)
    dev=x.sum()-0.5*n
    if nu: g += nu*dev
    return g
```

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 try: h_best=np.asarray(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n_best=len(h_best)
 except: h_best=np.full(500,0.5); n_best=500
 h_best=project_box_sum(h_best,n_best/2)
 q=compute_q(h_best); best_raw=q.max(); best_c=best_raw*2/n_best
 # Maybe quick exact polish first (small passes) to improve parent.
 coords_best=build_coords(n_best)
 h_best, rawBest = exact_cd(h_best,n_best,deadline-600,coords_best,passes=20,chunk=n_best//5,rng=rng)
 best_raw=rawBest; best_c=best_raw*2/n_best
 # L-BFGS refine on n_best with time limit maybe 400s
 if time < deadline-300:
    h_l, raw_l = lbfgs_refine(h_best,n_best, alphas=[20,80,300,1200,5000], deadline=deadline-250, maxiter=120)
    if raw_l*2/n_best < best_c: update
 # CD after L-BFGS
 if time < deadline-150:
    coords=build_coords(n_best) if n changed else coords_best
    h_c, raw_c = exact_cd(h_best,n_best,deadline-140,coords,passes=30,chunk=n_best//5,rng=rng)
    update
 # Symmetric variant
 if time < deadline-150:
    h_sym=0.5*(h_best + h_best[::-1]); project; maybe if different; run short lbfgs+cd at time 80s; update
 # Low-res variants
 for n_low in [180,240,320]:
    if time < deadline-120:
       h_low=interp_h(h_best,n_best,n_low)
       coords_low=build_coords(n_low)
       hw, raww = exact_cd(h_low,n_low,deadline-120,coords_low,passes=100,chunk=max(20,n_low//4),rng=rng)
       c5w=raww*2/n_low
       if c5w < best_c - 1e-9:
           # upsample to n_best? or keep low? If low is better, we can return low. But maybe high-res can improve further.
           # Run short CD at high from upsample, if improves keep low or high.
           h_hi=interp_h(hw,n_low,n_best); h_hi=project...
           coords_hi=build_coords(n_best)
           hh, rawh=exact_cd(h_hi,n_best,deadline-100,coords_hi,passes=20)
           if rawh*2/n_best < best_c: update high
           else: update low (hw, n_low) because lower.
           # Could also L-BFGS low? Time.
 # Final polish on best
 if time < deadline-5:
    coords=build_coords(n_best) (if n maybe changed) 
    hf, rawf = exact_cd(h_best,n_best,deadline,coords,passes=40,chunk=n_best//5)
    update
 # final project exact
 h_best=np.clip(h_best,0,1); project...
 best_c=compute_c5...
 return h_best,best_c,n_best
```

Let's implement `lbfgs_refine` with time allocation. To avoid many minimize calls, use alphas list and maybe one nu. We can adapt: if raw no improve after several alphas, break? Maybe keep.

```
def lbfgs_refine(h0, n, deadline, alphas, nu=200.0, maxiter=150):
   h=h0.copy(); best_raw=max(q)
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   for alpha in alphas:
      if time.time()>deadline-5: break
      try:
         res = minimize(obj_pen, h, jac=jac_pen, args=(n,alpha,nu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                        options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-18, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxls':12, 'maxcor':8})
         x=res.x if res.success else h
         # If res failed but x may be better
         x2=project_box_sum(x,0.5*n)
         raw2=max(compute_q(x2))
         if raw2 < best_raw - 1e-9:
             h=x2; best_raw=raw2
      except: pass
   return h,best_raw
```
Maybe use `nu` larger if h already feasible. `nu=100`.

`project_box_sum` bisection details: If x has large out of bounds, lo/hi. T=n/2. We need exact target. Bisection 80 gives 1e-24? 80 enough. But each iteration np.clip. For many CD chunks, 60 maybe. Let's implement:
```
def project_box_sum(x, target):
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   n=x.size
   if abs(x.sum()-target)<1e-13 and np.all(x>=-1e-12) and np.all(x<=1+1e-12):
      y=np.clip(x,0,1); ... adjust tiny
   lo = -np.max(x) - 1.0
   hi = 1.0 - np.min(x) + 1.0
   # ensure sum at lo<target, hi>target
   for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(x+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
   lam=(lo+hi)/2
   y=np.clip(x+lam,0,1)
   diff=target-y.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-10:
      if diff>0:
         room=1-y
         idx=np.where(room>1e-10)[0]
         if idx.size:
            total=room[idx].sum()
            # distribute limited by room; one loop? Since lam near projection, residual tiny.
            for _ in range(10):
               add=diff/idx.size
               y[idx]+=add
               over=y[idx]>1
               if over.any(): ...
      else similar
   else diffuse tiny
   return y
```
But bisection 80 over all chunks heavy? 80*700=56k per projection, not heavy. But 80 lo/hi wide may require? okay. Use 70.

We can optimize target sum by returning exactly after bisection to ~1e-14; residual due float sum. We can adjust one coordinate with residual if within bounds. Better than loop:
```
diff=target-y.sum()
if abs(diff)>0:
   # find index with room if diff>0, else mass
   if diff>0:
       idx=np.argmax(1-y)
       add=min(diff,1-y[idx])
       y[idx]+=add
       diff-=add
   else:
       idx=np.argmin(y); add=max(diff,y[idx]); y[idx]+=add (negative)
   # if still residual (if target diff large, distribute)
```

```
def compute_q(h): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
def compute_c5(h,n): return float(np.max(compute_q(h)) * (2.0/n))

Build coords top-level. Store arrays. For each p, the range length total maybe ~? We can store lists. For pos/neg local, after removing diag.
```
def build_coords(n):
  N=n-1; coords=[]
  for p in range(n):
    lo = p if p < N-p else N-p
    hi = N+p if N+p > 2*N-p else 2*N-p
    ms = np.arange(lo, hi+1, dtype=int)
    d = N - ms
    first = (p+d >=0) & (p+d < n)
    second = (p-d >=0) & (p-d < n)
    pos = np.where(first)[0]; neg=np.where(second)[0]
    diag_arr = np.where(ms == N)[0]
    diag = int(diag_arr[0]) if diag_arr.size else -1
    if diag >=0:
        pos = pos[pos != diag]
        neg = neg[neg != diag]
    gpos = p + d[pos]
    gneg = p - d[neg]
    coords.append((lo,hi,ms,pos,gpos,neg,gneg,diag))
  return coords
```
`lo` min, `hi` max. For n=1? n_points maybe >=50. Assume >1.

`diag_local` is local index within ms. `ms[diag]` global lag. Good.

    new = pick_x(xl,xh,old,U,has_diag)
    return new, ms, pos_l, gpos, neg_l, gneg, c_diag?

def pick_x(xl,xh,old,U,has_diag):

After updating h[p], c updated exactly? Let's verify diagonal: `c[ms[diag]] += new*(1-new)-old*(1-old)`. Non-diag: For pos structural, term old*(1-other) to new: increase by (new-old)*(1-other). What if p appears in q_m as both first and second but different i? For term structure q_m=∑ h_i(1-h_{i+d}); a fixed p can be i=p (first) for some i, and j=i+d=p for other i. Both conditions require d=0 for i=p? Suppose p as first with i=p; as second with i=p-d. If both i valid and d≠0, then term p appears in two separate pairs in the same q_m: one as first factor with i=p and another as second factor with i=p-d? Wait q_m is a sum over i. p can appear as h_i in term i=p and in (1-h_{i+d}) in term i=p-d; these are two different terms if d≠0. Both can be valid! Ah! I earlier thought only diagonal both, but for a fixed shift d, coordinate p can be the unshifted point in one pair (i=p) and the shifted point in another pair (i=p-d) simultaneously because the sum includes all i. For q_d=∑_{i} h_i(1-h_{i+d}), the variable h_p appears in factor h_i when i=p, and in factor (1-h_{i+d}) when i=p-d. These are two different summands (unless d=0 where same summand). ```

Project:
```
def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = x.size
    # simple if already
    # bisection
    minx = float(np.min(x)); maxx=float(np.max(x))
    lo = -maxx - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - minx + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.clip(x+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    y=np.clip(x+(lo+hi)*0.5,0.0,1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    # adjust residual
    if diff > 0:
       order = np.argsort(1.0 - y)
       # add to largest room first? if diff tiny, any. Use product? We'll loop.
       for _ in range(10):
           idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y))
           room = 1.0 - y[idx]
           add = min(diff, room)
           y[idx] += add
           diff -= add
           if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
    elif diff < 0:
       for _ in range(10):
           idx=int(np.argmin(y))
           room = y[idx]
           add = max(diff, -room)
           y[idx] += add
           diff -= add
           if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
    if y.sum() != target:
       # last resort distribute?
       idx=np.argsort(1-y if diff>0 else y)
       # but diff should ~0
    return y
```
The residual adjustment if diff tiny but at bound? Since bisection should produce sum target within float; diff may be e.g. 1e-14. If the index with largest room has room not enough? only if diff large due bisection fail. Fine.

But `np.argmax(1-y)` chooses bound closest to 1; if diff tiny okay. It may increase y[idx] slightly beyond 1 if residual >room? we min. If diff remains, loop up to 10 may not fix if bisection wrong. But bisection 70 should be fine. Could simpler final: adjust all off-bound by distributing residual: as previous. But okay.

Compute q/c5:
```
def compute_q(h): return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
def compute_c5(h,n): return float(np.max(compute_q(h)) * (2.0 / n))
```

Build coords as above.

Pick_x:
```
def pick_x(xl,xh,old,U,has_diag):
   if xl > xh: return old
   if xl < 0: xl=0.0
   if xh > 1: xh=1.0
   if xl > xh: return old
   if has_diag and U < 0.25 - 1e-12:
      disc=1.0-4.0*U
      if disc<0: disc=0.0
      sq=math.sqrt(disc)
      r1=0.5*(1.0-sq); r2=0.5*(1.0+sq)
      # if old feasible
      if xl-1e-11 <= old <= xh+1e-11 and (old <= r1+1e-11 or old >= r2-1e-11):
          return float(np.clip(old,0.0,1.0))
      cands=[]
      c1 = r1
      if c1 > xh: c1=xh
      if c1 >= xl-1e-12: cands.append(c1)
      c2 = r2
      if c2 < xl: c2=xl
      if c2 <= xh+1e-12: cands.append(c2)
      if not cands:
          # choose clip old if outside
          return float(np.clip(old,xl,xh)) if xl<=xh else old
      # choose closest to old
      bestv=cands[0]; bestd=abs(bestv-old)
      for v in cands[1:]:
         dd=abs(v-old)
         if dd<bestd: bestd=dd; bestv=v
      return float(np.clip(bestv,0.0,1.0))
   # no hole
   if xl-1e-11 <= old <= xh+1e-11:
      return float(np.clip(old,0.0,1.0))
   return float(np.clip(old,xl,xh))
```
If old outside and all feasible, clip to boundary. Good.

Solve_coord:
```
def solve_coord(c,h,p,co):
    lo,hi,ms,pos_l,gpos,neg_l,gneg,diag=co
    old=float(h[p])
    cb=c[ms]
    K=len(ms)
    a=np.zeros(K, dtype=float)
    if pos_l.size:
       a[pos_l] += 1.0 - h[gpos]
    if neg_l.size:
       a[neg_l] -= h[gneg]
    has_diag = diag >= 0
    if has_diag:
       c_diag = float(cb[diag])
       a[diag] = 0.0
    else:
       c_diag = 0.0
    # outside max
    unaff = 0.0 # since q nonnegative; but if c can be negative? no. Use 0.
    if lo > 0:
       m = float(np.max(c[:lo]));
       if m > unaff: unaff=m
    if hi + 1 < c.size:
       m = float(np.max(c[hi+1:]))
       if m > unaff: unaff=m
    low = unaff
    if low < 0: low=0.0
    # zero non-diag
    zero_idx = np.where(np.abs(a) <= 1e-13)[0]
    if has_diag and diag in zero_idx:
       zero_idx = zero_idx[zero_idx != diag]
    if zero_idx.size:
       mz = float(np.max(cb[zero_idx]))
       if mz > low: low = mz
    # pos/neg arrays
    pos_idx = np.where(a > 1e-13)[0]
    neg_idx = np.where(a < -1e-13)[0]
    p_a = a[pos_idx]; p_cb = cb[pos_idx]
    n_a = a[neg_idx]; n_cb = cb[neg_idx]
    npos = p_a.size; nneg = n_a.size
    high = float(np.max(c))
    if high < low: high = low
    if high <= low + 1e-13:
       return old, ms, a, diag
    # binary
    for _ in range(34):
       L=(low+high)*0.5
       xl=0.0; xh=1.0
       if npos:
          upp=float(np.min((L - p_cb) / p_a + old))
          if upp < xh: xh=upp
       if nneg:
          lowb=float(np.max((L - n_cb) / n_a + old))
          if lowb > xl: xl=lowb
       if xl < 0.0: xl=0.0
       if xh > 1.0: xh=1.0
       ok = (xl <= xh + 1e-12)
       if ok and has_diag:
          U = L - c_diag + old*(1.0-old)
          if U < -1e-12:
             ok=False
          elif U < 0.25 - 1e-14:
             disc=1.0-4.0*U
             if disc < 0: disc=0.0
             sq=math.sqrt(disc)
             r1=0.5*(1.0-sq); r2=0.5*(1.0+sq)
             if xl > r1 + 1e-12 and xh < r2 - 1e-12:
                ok=False
       if ok: high=L
       else: low=L
    L=high
    xl=0.0; xh=1.0
    if npos:
       xh=min(xh, float(np.min((L - p_cb)/p_a + old)))
    if nneg:
       xl=max(xl, float(np.max((L - n_cb)/n_a + old)))
    xl=max(0.0,xl); xh=min(1.0,xh)
    U = L - c_diag + old*(1.0-old) if has_diag else 1.0
    new = pick_x(xl,xh,old,U,has_diag)
    return new, ms, a, diag
```
Note `zero_idx = np.where` creates array; `if diag in zero_idx` uses Python loop maybe O(K) but K small; can use `zero_idx = zero_idx[zero_idx != diag]` only if has_diag. `diag in zero_idx` okay. We can simplify:
```
if has_diag:
   zero_idx = zero_idx[zero_idx != diag]
```
No `in` first. Good.

Potential bug: If a at diagonal was set 0, then `pos_idx/neg_idx` computed after, excludes diag. Good.

`zero_idx` includes lags with a=0 due cancellation. low includes their max. But what if there is a zero-influence lag with `cb` > high? impossible cb slice of c. high max c. ok.

`high` should be `max(c)` maybe if c has negative? no.

Now apply_update:
```
def apply_update(c, co, a, old, new):
    delta = new - old
    if abs(delta) <= 1e-15: return
    lo,hi,ms,pos_l,gpos,neg_l,gneg,diag=co
    if diag >= 0:
       mask = np.ones(ms.size, dtype=bool)
       mask[diag] = False
       c[ms[mask]] += a[mask] * delta
       c[ms[diag]] += new*(1.0-new) - old*(1.0-old)
    else:
       c[ms] += a * delta
```
This uses `co` but only ms,diag.

One nuance: If there are multiple affected lags and `a` for zero lags 0, applying no change. Good.

`exact_cd` with precomputed coords. Need target. Use chunk. Use `int(max(1, n//7))` maybe.
```
def exact_cd(h,n,deadline,coords,passes,chunk,rng):
    h=h.copy()
    target=0.5*n
    c=compute_q(h)
    best_raw=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy()
    if n <= 1: return h,best_raw
    chunk=int(chunk)
    if chunk<1: chunk=1
    order=list(range(n))
    stall=0
    for it in range(passes):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        if rng is not None and it % 2 == 1:
            rng.shuffle(order)
        for start in range(0,n,chunk):
            stop=min(start+chunk,n)
            for p in order[start:stop]:
                co=coords[p]
                old=float(h[p])
                new, ms, a, diag = solve_coord(c,h,p,co)
                if abs(new-old) > 1e-12:
                    apply_update(c,co,a,old,new)
                    h[p]=new
            if abs(float(h.sum()) - target) > 1e-7:
                h = project_box_sum(h, target)
                c = compute_q(h)
            # else maybe small drift; no recompute to save
        cur_raw=float(c.max())
        if cur_raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
            best_raw=cur_raw; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 6: break
    # final project to target
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    c=compute_q(h)
    best_raw=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h, best_raw
```
After each pass, if projection not called because sum drift small, c may be slightly inconsistent with h due drift? Wait if we update coordinate p but sum drifts, c update formulas valid for h as updated, regardless of sum. So c consistent with h. Projection condition only if sum off >1e-7 to force mass; if not, c consistent. But exact objective should be with mass constrained; if sum drifts small, c consistent but mass slightly off. It's okay for temporary. At end project/recompute.

However within a chunk, after several coordinate updates, sum drift maybe >1e-7; we don't check until end of chunk. That's okay. At end, if drift >1e-7, projection recompute. If drift small, c consistent. Good.

Could `h[p]` go outside [0,1]? solve pick clips; yes.

If `new` differs but applying c with old h, and h[p] not updated yet; a coefficients computed before. Good. Then `h[p]=new`.

Now `lbfgs_refine`:
```
def smooth_value(x,n,alpha):
    q=compute_q(x)
    mx=float(q.max())
    u=alpha*(q-mx)
    s=float(np.exp(u).sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
       return mx
    return mx + math.log(s)/alpha

def obj_pen(x,n,alpha,nu):
    val=smooth_value(x,n,alpha)
    dev=x.sum()-0.5*n
    return val + 0.5*nu*dev*dev

def smooth_grad(x,n,alpha):
    q=compute_q(x)
    mx=float(q.max())
    u=alpha*(q-mx)
    p=np.exp(u)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 0.0:
       p = np.zeros_like(p); p[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
    else: p /= s
    w = p[::-1]
    N=n-1
    ones=np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    # if n? 
    A = np.convolve(w, ones, 'full')[N:N+n]
    D1 = np.convolve(w, x, 'full')[N:N+n]
    D2 = np.convolve(w[::-1], x, 'full')[N:N+n]
    return A - D1 - D2

def jac_pen(x,n,alpha,nu):
    g=smooth_grad(x,n,alpha)
    if nu:
       dev=x.sum()-0.5*n
       g = g + nu*dev
    return g
```
Adding scalar `nu*dev` to array works (broadcast). Good.

`w[::-1]` view; if `w` is p[::-1], then w[::-1]=p? Could use p to save: D2 = convolve(p, x, full). Actually w[::-1] is p original m order? Wait p order m, w d order. Formula D2=∑w_d h[j+d]. Since w[::-1] is p order? Let's just use `w.reverse copy`: `np.convolve(w[::-1], x, 'full')`. That's p? p[::-1]? If w=p[::-1], w[::-1]=p. Yes. We can just use p for D2 to avoid another reverse? `lbfgs_refine`:
```
def lbfgs_refine(h0,n,deadline,alphas,nu,maxiter):
    h=h0.copy()
    target=0.5*n
    best_raw=float(np.max(compute_q(h)))
    _TIME_LIMIT[0]=deadline
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 3: break
        try:
           res = minimize(obj_pen, h, jac=jac_pen, args=(n,alpha,nu), method='L-BFGS-B',
                          bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-10,'maxls':10,'maxcor':8}, callback=_cb)
           x = res.x if hasattr(res,'x') else h
           if x is None: x=h
           # check bounds? minimize should
           x = np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=float),0,1)
           x = project_box_sum(x,target)
           raw=float(np.max(compute_q(x)))
           if raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
               h=x; best_raw=raw
        except Exception:
           pass
    return h,best_raw
```
Need ensure `_TIME_LIMIT` set for all calls. callback returns True if expired; L-BFGS may stop. Good.

What `nu`? Use 200. We can optionally run two nu values if time. In run, call once with nu=300 maybe.

 interp:
```
def interp_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old,endpoint=False)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new,endpoint=False)
    hnew=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h,left=float(h[-1]),right=float(h[0]))
    return project_box_sum(hnew,0.5*n_new)
```
If n_old=1? not.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):

 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 try: h_best=np.asarray(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n_best=len(h_best)
 except Exception: h_best=np.full(350,0.5); n_best=350

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_TIME_LIMIT = [1e300]

def _callback(x):
    if time.time() > _TIME_LIMIT[0]:
        return True
    return False

def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    minx = float(np.min(x))
    maxx = float(np.max(x))
    lo = -maxx - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - minx + 1.0
    for _ in range(72):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum() < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(12):
        diff = target - float(y.sum())
        if diff > 1e-13:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y))
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - y[idx])
            y[idx] += add
        elif diff < -1e-13:
            idx = int(np.argmin(y))
            add = max(diff, -y[idx])
            y[idx] += add
        else:
            break
    return y

def compute_q(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(compute_q(h)) * (2.0 / n))

def build_coords(n):
    N = n - 1
    coords = []
    for p in range(n):
        lo = p if p < N - p else N - p
        hi = N + p if N + p > 2 * N - p else 2 * N - p
        ms = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=int)
        d = N - ms
        first = (p + d >= 0) & (p + d < n)
        second = (p - d >= 0) & (p - d < n)
        pos_l = np.where(first)[0]
        neg_l = np.where(second)[0]
        diag_arr = np.where(ms == N)[0]
        diag = int(diag_arr[0]) if diag_arr.size else -1
        if diag >= 0:
            pos_l = pos_l[pos_l != diag]
            neg_l = neg_l[neg_l != diag]
        gpos = p + d[pos_l]
        gneg = p - d[neg_l]
        coords.append((lo, hi, ms, pos_l, gpos, neg_l, gneg, diag))
    return coords

def pick_x(xl, xh, old, U, has_diag):
    if xl > xh:
        return old
    if xl < 0.0:
        xl = 0.0
    if xh > 1.0:
        xh = 1.0
    if xl > xh:
        return old
    if has_diag and U < 0.25 - 1e-12:
        disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * U
        if disc < 0.0:
            disc = 0.0
        sq = math.sqrt(disc)
        r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - sq)
        r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + sq)
        if xl - 1e-11 <= old <= xh + 1e-11 and (old <= r1 + 1e-11 or old >= r2 - 1e-11):
            return float(np.clip(old, 0.0, 1.0))
        cands = []
        c1 = min(r1, xh)
        if c1 >= xl - 1e-12:
            cands.append(c1)
        c2 = max(r2, xl)
        if c2 <= xh + 1e-12:
            cands.append(c2)
        if not cands:
            return float(np.clip(old, xl, xh))
        best = cands[0]
        bd = abs(best - old)
        for v in cands[1:]:
            d = abs(v - old)
            if d < bd:
                bd = d
                best = v
        return float(np.clip(best, 0.0, 1.0))
    if xl - 1e-11 <= old <= xh + 1e-11:
        return float(np.clip(old, 0.0, 1.0))
    return float(np.clip(old, xl, xh))

def solve_coord(c, h, p, co):
    lo, hi, ms, pos_l, gpos, neg_l, gneg, diag = co
    old = float(h[p])
    cb = c[ms]
    a = np.zeros(ms.size, dtype=float)
    if pos_l.size:
        a[pos_l] += 1.0 - h[gpos]
    if neg_l.size:
        a[neg_l] -= h[gneg]
    has_diag = diag >= 0
    if has_diag:
        c_diag = float(cb[diag])
        a[diag] = 0.0
    else:
        c_diag = 0.0
    low = 0.0
    if lo > 0:
        v = float(np.max(c[:lo]))
        if v > low:
            low = v
    if hi + 1 < c.size:
        v = float(np.max(c[hi + 1:]))
        if v > low:
            low = v
    z = np.where(np.abs(a) <= 1e-13)[0]
    if has_diag and z.size:
        z = z[z != diag]
    if z.size:
        v = float(np.max(cb[z]))
        if v > low:
            low = v
    pidx = np.where(a > 1e-13)[0]
    nidx = np.where(a < -1e-13)[0]
    pa = a[pidx]
    pcb = cb[pidx]
    na = a[nidx]
    ncb = cb[nidx]
    npos = pa.size
    nneg = na.size
    high = float(np.max(c))
    if high < low:
        high = low
    if high <= low + 1e-13:
        return old, ms, a, diag
    for _ in range(36):
        L = 0.5 * (low + high)
        xl = 0.0
        xh = 1.0
        if npos:
            v = float(np.min((L - pcb) / pa + old))
            if v < xh:
                xh = v
        if nneg:
            v = float(np.max((L - ncb) / na + old))
            if v > xl:
                xl = v
        if xl < 0.0:
            xl = 0.0
        if xh > 1.0:
            xh = 1.0
        ok = (xl <= xh + 1e-12)
        if ok and has_diag:
            U = L - c_diag + old * (1.0 - old)
            if U < -1e-12:
                ok = False
            elif U < 0.25 - 1e-14:
                disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * U
                if disc < 0.0:
                    disc = 0.0
                sq = math.sqrt(disc)
                r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - sq)
                r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + sq)
                if xl > r1 + 1e-12 and xh < r2 - 1e-12:
                    ok = False
        if ok:
            high = L
        else:
            low = L
    L = high
    xl = 0.0
    xh = 1.0
    if npos:
        v = float(np.min((L - pcb) / pa + old))
        if v < xh:
            xh = v
    if nneg:
        v = float(np.max((L - ncb) / na + old))
        if v > xl:
            xl = v
    xl = max(0.0, xl)
    xh = min(1.0, xh)
    U = L - c_diag + old * (1.0 - old) if has_diag else 1.0
    newv = pick_x(xl, xh, old, U, has_diag)
    return newv, ms, a, diag

def apply_update(c, co, a, old, newv):
    delta = newv - old
    if abs(delta) <= 1e-15:
        return
    lo, hi, ms, pos_l, gpos, neg_l, gneg, diag = co
    if diag >= 0:
        mask = np.ones(ms.size, dtype=bool)
        mask[diag] = False
        c[ms[mask]] += a[mask] * delta
        c[ms[diag]] += newv * (1.0 - newv) - old * (1.0 - old)
    else:
        c[ms] += a * delta

def exact_cd(h, n, deadline, coords, passes, chunk, rng):
    h = h.copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    c = compute_q(h)
    best_raw = float(np.max(c))
    best_h = h.copy()
    order = list(range(n))
    if chunk < 1:
        chunk = 1
    stall = 0
    for it in range(passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if rng is not None and it % 2 == 1:
            rng.shuffle(order)
        for start in range(0, n, chunk):
            for p in order[start:start + chunk]:
                co = coords[p]
                old = float(h[p])
                newv, ms, a, diag = solve_coord(c, h, p, co)
                if abs(newv - old) > 1e-12:
                    apply_update(c, co, a, old, newv)
                    h[p] = newv
            if abs(float(h.sum()) - target) > 1e-6:
                h = project_box_sum(h, target)
                c = compute_q(h)
        h = project_box_sum(h, target)
        c = compute_q(h)
        cur_raw = float(np.max(c))
        if cur_raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
            best_raw = cur_raw
            best_h = h.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 8:
                break
    return best_h, best_raw

def smooth_value(x, n, alpha):
    q = compute_q(x)
    mx = float(np.max(q))
    u = alpha * (q - mx)
    s = float(np.exp(u).sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return mx
    return mx + math.log(s) / alpha

def obj_pen(x, n, alpha, nu):
    v = smooth_value(x, n, alpha)
    dev = float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n
    return v + 0.5 * nu * dev * dev

def smooth_grad(x, n, alpha):
    q = compute_q(x)
    mx = float(np.max(q))
    u = alpha * (q - mx)
    p = np.exp(u)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros_like(p)
        p[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
    else:
        p /= s
    w = p[::-1]
    N = n - 1
    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    A = np.convolve(w, ones, "full")[N:N + n]
    D1 = np.convolve(w, x, "full")[N:N + n]
    D2 = np.convolve(p, x, "full")[N:N + n]
    return A - D1 - D2

def jac_pen(x, n, alpha, nu):
    g = smooth_grad(x, n, alpha)
    if nu:
        g = g + nu * (float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n)
    return g

def lbfgs_refine(h0, n, deadline, alphas, nu, maxiter):
    h = h0.copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    best_raw = float(np.max(compute_q(h)))
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    _TIME_LIMIT[0] = deadline
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj_pen,
                h,
                jac=jac_pen,
                args=(n, alpha, nu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "ftol": 1e-18,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxls": 10,
                    "maxcor": 8,
                },
                callback=_callback,
            )
            x = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
            x = project_box_sum(x, target)
            raw = float(np.max(compute_q(x)))
            if raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
                h = x
                best_raw = raw
        except Exception:
            pass
    return h, best_raw

def interp_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    y = np.interp(xn, xo, h, left=float(h[-1]), right=float(h[0]))
    return project_box_sum(y, 0.5 * n_new)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 4.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h_best = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n_best = int(len(h_best))
    except Exception:
        h_best = np.full(350, 0.5)
        n_best = 350
    h_best = project_box_sum(h_best, 0.5 * n_best)
    best_raw = float(np.max(compute_q(h_best)))
    best_c = best_raw * 2.0 / n_best
    n_start = n_best

    coords = build_coords(n_best)
    dl = min(deadline, time.time() + 140.0)
    h1, raw1 = exact_cd(h_best, n_best, dl, coords, 45, max(40, n_best // 7), rng)
    if raw1 * 2.0 / n_best < best_c:
        best_c = raw1 * 2.0 / n_best
        best_raw = raw1
        h_best = h1

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 160.0:
        lbdl = time.time() + min(360.0, rem - 160.0)
        h2, raw2 = lbfgs_refine(h_best, n_best, lbdl, [20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0], 300.0, 60)
        if raw2 * 2.0 / n_best < best_c:
            best_c = raw2 * 2.0 / n_best
            best_raw = raw2
            h_best = h2

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 90.0:
        dl = min(deadline, time.time() + min(260.0, rem - 90.0))
        coords = build_coords(n_best)
        h3, raw3 = exact_cd(h_best, n_best, dl, coords, 80, max(40, n_best // 7), rng)
        if raw3 * 2.0 / n_best < best_c:
            best_c = raw3 * 2.0 / n_best
            best_raw = raw3
            h_best = h3

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 120.0:
        hs = 0.5 * (h_best + h_best[::-1])
        hs = project_box_sum(hs, 0.5 * n_best)
        coords = build_coords(n_best)
        dl = min(deadline, time.time() + min(80.0, rem - 80.0))
        hs, raws = exact_cd(hs, n_best, dl, coords, 50, max(35, n_best // 7), rng)
        if raws * 2.0 / n_best < best_c:
            best_c = raws * 2.0 / n_best
            best_raw = raws
            h_best = hs
        if time.time() < deadline - 45.0:
            dl = min(deadline, time.time() + min(55.0, max(20.0, deadline - time.time() - 35.0)))
            hs2, raws2 = lbfgs_refine(hs, n_best, dl, [100.0, 500.0, 2000.0], 300.0, 35)
            if raws2 * 2.0 / n_best < best_c:
                best_c = raws2 * 2.0 / n_best
                best_raw = raws2
                h_best = hs2

    for nl in (180, 240, 320):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 130.0:
            break
        hl = interp_h(h_best, n_best, nl)
        cl = build_coords(nl)
        dl = min(deadline, time.time() + min(110.0, rem - 70.0))
        hw, raww = exact_cd(hl, nl, dl, cl, 250, max(20, nl // 5), rng)
        c5w = raww * 2.0 / nl
        if c5w < best_c - 1e-9:
            h_best = hw
            n_best = nl
            best_raw = raww
            best_c = c5w
            if n_start > nl and deadline - time.time() > 110.0:
                hh = interp_h(h_best, nl, n_start)
                chh = build_coords(n_start)
                dh = min(deadline, time.time() + min(90.0, max(30.0, deadline - time.time() - 50.0)))
                hh, rawh = exact_cd(hh, n_start, dh, chh, 45, max(40, n_start // 7), rng)
                c5h = rawh * 2.0 / n_start
                if c5h < best_c:
                    h_best = hh
                    n_best = n_start
                    best_raw = rawh
                    best_c = c5h
            break

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 25.0:
        coords = build_coords(n_best)
        dl = min(deadline, time.time() + min(120.0, rem - 5.0))
        hf, rawf = exact_cd(h_best, n_best, dl, coords, 100, max(35, n_best // 7), rng)
        if rawf * 2.0 / n_best < best_c:
            best_c = rawf * 2.0 / n_best
            best_raw = rawf
            h_best = hf

    h_best = np.clip(h_best, 0.0, 1.0)
    h_best = project_box_sum(h_best, 0.5 * n_best)
    best_raw = float(np.max(compute_q(h_best)))
    best_c = best_raw * 2.0 / n_best
    return h_best, best_c, n_best
```
